  <script>
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
      const url = window.location.href

      // Check if the URL contains the "theme" parameter
      const themeParam = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("theme")
      const dirRTL = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("rtl")

      // If a theme parameter is present, apply it to the document
      if (themeParam) {
        document.documentElement.setAttribute("data-theme", themeParam)
      }
      if (dirRTL === "true") {
        document.documentElement.setAttribute("dir", "rtl")
      }
    })
  </script>
  

</body>

      .apexcharts-legend {

      .apexcharts-legend.apexcharts-legend-group-horizontal {
        flex-direction: column;
      }
      .apexcharts-legend-group {
        display: flex;

      .apexcharts-legend-group-vertical {
        flex-direction: column-reverse;
      }
      .apexcharts-legend.apx-legend-position-bottom, .apexcharts-legend.apx-legend-position-top {
        flex-wrap: wrap

      .apexcharts-legend.apx-legend-position-right, .apexcharts-legend.apx-legend-position-left {

      .apexcharts-legend-text {

    <script>
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
      const url = window.location.href

      // Check if the URL contains the "theme" parameter
      const themeParam = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("theme")
      const dirRTL = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("rtl")

      // If a theme parameter is present, apply it to the document
      if (themeParam) {
        document.documentElement.setAttribute("data-theme", themeParam)
      }
      if (dirRTL === "true") {
        document.documentElement.setAttribute("dir", "rtl")
      }
    })
  </script>

  <script>
    window.addEventListener("load", () => {
      ;(function () {
        // Order Area Chart with gradient
        buildChart("#order-chart", () => ({
          chart: {
            height: 104,
            type: "area",
            toolbar: {
              show: false
            },
            sparkline: {
              enabled: true
            }
          },
          markers: {
            size: 6,
            colors: "transparent",
            strokeColors: "transparent",
            strokeWidth: 4,
            discrete: [
              {
                fillColor: "var(--color-base-100)",
                seriesIndex: 0,
                dataPointIndex: 6,
                strokeColor: "var(--color-success)",
                strokeWidth: 2,
                size: 5,
                radius: 8
              }
            ],
            hover: {
              size: 7
            }
          },
          grid: {
            show: false,
            padding: {
              top: 15,
              right: 7,
              left: 0,
              bottom: 0
            }
          },
          colors: ["var(--color-success)"],
          fill: {
            type: "gradient",
            gradient: {
              shadeIntensity: 1,
              opacityFrom: 0.4,
              gradientToColors: ["var(--color-base-100)"],
              opacityTo: 0.1,
              stops: [0, 100]
            }
          },
          dataLabels: {
            enabled: false
          },
          stroke: {
            width: 2,
            curve: "smooth"
          },
          series: [
            {
              name: "Order",
              data: [180, 175, 275, 140, 205, 190, 295]
            }
          ],
          xaxis: {
            show: false,
            lines: {
              show: false
            },
            labels: {
              show: false
            },
            axisBorder: {
              show: false
            }
          },
          yaxis: {
            show: false
          }
        }))

        // Revenue Bar Chart
        buildChart("#revenue-chart", () => ({
          chart: {
            height: 104,
            type: "bar",
            toolbar: {
              show: false
            },
            sparkline: {
              enabled: false
            }
          },
          plotOptions: {
            bar: {
              barHeight: "80%",
              columnWidth: "54%",
              startingShape: "rounded",
              endingShape: "rounded",
              borderRadius: 2,
              distributed: true
            }
          },
          grid: {
            show: false,
            padding: {
              top: -20,
              bottom: -12,
              left: -10,
              right: 0
            }
          },
          colors: [
            "color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-primary) 10%, transparent)",
            "color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-primary) 10%, transparent)",
            "color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-primary) 10%, transparent)",
            "color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-primary) 10%, transparent)",
            "var(--color-primary)",
            "color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-primary) 10%, transparent)",
            "color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-primary) 10%, transparent)"
          ],
          dataLabels: {
            enabled: false
          },
          series: [
            {
              name: "Revenue",
              data: [40, 95, 60, 45, 90, 50, 75]
            }
          ],
          states: {
            hover: {
              filter: {
                type: "none"
              }
            },
            active: {
              filter: {
                type: "none"
              }
            }
          },
          legend: {
            show: false
          },
          xaxis: {
            categories: ["M", "T", "W", "T", "F", "S", "S"],
            axisBorder: {
              show: false
            },
            axisTicks: {
              show: false
            },
            labels: {
              style: {
                colors: "color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-base-content) 60%, transparent)",
                fontSize: "13px",
                fontFamily: "Inter, ui-sans-serif"
              }
            }
          },
          yaxis: {
            labels: {
              show: false
            }
          }
        }))

        // Expenses Radial Chart
        buildChart("#expenses-chart", () => ({
          chart: {
            height: 140,
            width: 140,
            sparkline: {
              enabled: true
            },
            parentHeightOffset: 0,
            type: "radialBar"
          },
          colors: ["var(--color-primary)"],
          series: [78],
          plotOptions: {
            radialBar: {
              startAngle: -90,
              endAngle: 90,
              hollow: {
                size: "60%"
              },
              track: {
                background: "color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-base-content) 10%, transparent)"
              },
              dataLabels: {
                name: {
                  show: false
                },
                value: {
                  fontSize: "18px",
                  fontFamily: "Inter, ui-sans-serif",
                  color: "var(--color-base-content)",
                  fontWeight: 600,
                  offsetY: -5
                }
              }
            }
          },
          stroke: {
            lineCap: "round"
          },
          grid: {
            show: false,
            padding: {
              left: 0,
              right: 0,
              top: 0,
              bottom: 3
            }
          },
          labels: ["Progress"]
        }))

        // Sessions Line Chart
        buildChart("#sessions-chart", () => ({
          chart: {
            height: 104,
            type: "area",
            toolbar: {
              show: false
            },
            sparkline: {
              enabled: true
            }
          },
          markers: {
            size: 6,
            colors: "transparent",
            strokeColors: "transparent",
            strokeWidth: 4,
            discrete: [
              {
                fillColor: "var(--color-base-100)",
                seriesIndex: 0,
                dataPointIndex: 8,
                strokeColor: "var(--color-warning)",
                strokeWidth: 2,
                size: 5,
                radius: 8
              }
            ],
            hover: {
              size: 7
            }
          },
          grid: {
            show: false,
            padding: {
              right: 8
            }
          },
          colors: ["var(--color-warning)"],
          fill: {
            type: "gradient",
            gradient: {
              shadeIntensity: 1,
              opacityFrom: 0.4,
              gradientToColors: ["var(--color-base-100)"],
              opacityTo: 0.4,
              stops: [0, 85, 100]
            }
          },
          dataLabels: {
            enabled: false
          },
          stroke: {
            width: 2,
            curve: "straight"
          },
          series: [
            {
              name: "Sessions",
              data: [340, 340, 300, 300, 240, 240, 320, 320, 370]
            }
          ],
          xaxis: {
            show: false,
            lines: {
              show: false
            },
            labels: {
              show: false
            },
            axisBorder: {
              show: false
            }
          },
          yaxis: {
            show: false
          }
        }))

        // Profit Bar Chart
        buildChart("#profit-chart", () => ({
          chart: {
            type: "bar",
            height: 104,
            toolbar: {
              tools: {
                download: false
              }
            }
          },
          series: [
            {
              name: "Profit",
              data: [73, 73, 56, 56, 100]
            },
            {
              name: "Profit",
              data: [61, 61, 42, 74, 72]
            }
          ],
          plotOptions: {
            bar: {
              columnWidth: "75%",
              startingShape: "rounded",
              endingShape: "rounded",
              borderRadius: 3,
              dataLabels: {
                show: false
              }
            }
          },
          states: {
            hover: {
              filter: {
                type: "none"
              }
            },
            active: {
              filter: {
                type: "none"
              }
            }
          },
          grid: {
            show: false,
            padding: {
              top: -30,
              bottom: -12,
              left: 0,
              right: 0
            }
          },
          colors: ["var(--color-success)", "color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-success) 10%, transparent)"],
          dataLabels: {
            enabled: false
          },
          stroke: {
            show: true,
            width: 2,
            colors: ["var(--color-base-100)"]
          },
          legend: {
            show: false
          },
          xaxis: {
            categories: ["Jan", "Feb", "Mar", "Apr", "May"],
            axisBorder: {
              show: false
            },
            axisTicks: {
              show: false
            },
            labels: {
              style: {
                colors: "color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-base-content) 50%, transparent)",
                fontSize: "12px",
                fontFamily: "Inter, ui-sans-serif"
              }
            }
          },
          yaxis: {
            labels: {
              show: false
            }
          }
        }))

        // Sales Radial Chart
        buildChart("#sales-chart", () => ({
          chart: {
            height: 140,
            width: 140,
            sparkline: {
              enabled: true
            },
            parentHeightOffset: 0,
            type: "radialBar"
          },
          colors: ["var(--color-accent)"],
          series: [50],
          plotOptions: {
            radialBar: {
              startAngle: -90,
              endAngle: 90,
              hollow: {
                size: "60%"
              },
              track: {
                background: "color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-base-content) 10%, transparent)"
              },
              dataLabels: {
                name: {
                  show: false
                },
                value: {
                  fontSize: "18px",
                  fontFamily: "Inter, ui-sans-serif",
                  color: "var(--color-base-content)",
                  fontWeight: 600,
                  offsetY: -5
                }
              }
            }
          },
          stroke: {
            lineCap: "round"
          },
          grid: {
            show: false,
            padding: {
              left: 0,
              right: 0,
              top: 0,
              bottom: 3
            }
          },
          labels: ["Progress"]
        }))
      })()
    })
  </script>
  

</body>

            <div id="sales-chart" class="mb-2 flex justify-center" style="min-height: 73px;"><div id="apexchartsjunn2dwcj" class="apexcharts-canvas apexchartsjunn2dwcj apexcharts-theme-light" style="width: 140px; height: 73px;"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" version="1.1" xmlns:xlink="http://www.w3.org/1999/xlink" class="apexcharts-svg" xmlns:data="ApexChartsNS" transform="translate(0, 0)" width="140" height="73"><foreignObject x="0" y="0" width="140" height="73"><style type="text/css">
      .apexcharts-flip-y {
        transform: scaleY(-1) translateY(-100%);
        transform-origin: top;
        transform-box: fill-box;
      }
      .apexcharts-flip-x {
        transform: scaleX(-1);
        transform-origin: center;
        transform-box: fill-box;
      }
      .apexcharts-legend {
        display: flex;
        overflow: auto;
        padding: 0 10px;
      }
      .apexcharts-legend.apexcharts-legend-group-horizontal {
        flex-direction: column;
      }
      .apexcharts-legend-group {
        display: flex;
      }
      .apexcharts-legend-group-vertical {
        flex-direction: column-reverse;
      }
      .apexcharts-legend.apx-legend-position-bottom, .apexcharts-legend.apx-legend-position-top {
        flex-wrap: wrap
      }
      .apexcharts-legend.apx-legend-position-right, .apexcharts-legend.apx-legend-position-left {
        flex-direction: column;
        bottom: 0;
      }
      .apexcharts-legend.apx-legend-position-bottom.apexcharts-align-left, .apexcharts-legend.apx-legend-position-top.apexcharts-align-left, .apexcharts-legend.apx-legend-position-right, .apexcharts-legend.apx-legend-position-left {
        justify-content: flex-start;
        align-items: flex-start;
      }
      .apexcharts-legend.apx-legend-position-bottom.apexcharts-align-center, .apexcharts-legend.apx-legend-position-top.apexcharts-align-center {
        justify-content: center;
        align-items: center;
      }
      .apexcharts-legend.apx-legend-position-bottom.apexcharts-align-right, .apexcharts-legend.apx-legend-position-top.apexcharts-align-right {
        justify-content: flex-end;
        align-items: flex-end;
      }
      .apexcharts-legend-series {
        cursor: pointer;
        line-height: normal;
        display: flex;
        align-items: center;
      }
      .apexcharts-legend-text {
        position: relative;
        font-size: 14px;
      }
      .apexcharts-legend-text *, .apexcharts-legend-marker * {
        pointer-events: none;
      }
      .apexcharts-legend-marker {
        position: relative;
        display: flex;
        align-items: center;
        justify-content: center;
        cursor: pointer;
        margin-right: 1px;
      }

      .apexcharts-legend-series.apexcharts-no-click {
        cursor: auto;
      }
      .apexcharts-legend .apexcharts-hidden-zero-series, .apexcharts-legend .apexcharts-hidden-null-series {
        display: none !important;
      }
      .apexcharts-inactive-legend {
        opacity: 0.45;
      }

  <script src="https://flyonui.becdn.net/pro/libs/apexcharts/dist/apexcharts.min.js"></script>

    <script src="https://flyonui.becdn.net/pro/libs/flyonui/flyonui.js"></script>
    <script>
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
      const url = window.location.href

      // Check if the URL contains the "theme" parameter
      const themeParam = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("theme")
      const dirRTL = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("rtl")

      // If a theme parameter is present, apply it to the document
      if (themeParam) {
        document.documentElement.setAttribute("data-theme", themeParam)
      }
      if (dirRTL === "true") {
        document.documentElement.setAttribute("dir", "rtl")
      }
    })
  </script>

  <script>
    window.addEventListener("load", () => {
      ;(function () {
        // Order Area Chart with gradient
        buildChart("#order-chart", () => ({
          chart: {
            height: 104,
            type: "area",
            toolbar: {
              show: false
            },
            sparkline: {
              enabled: true
            }
          },
          markers: {
            size: 6,
            colors: "transparent",
            strokeColors: "transparent",
            strokeWidth: 4,
            discrete: [
              {
                fillColor: "var(--color-base-100)",
                seriesIndex: 0,
                dataPointIndex: 6,
                strokeColor: "var(--color-success)",
                strokeWidth: 2,

                radius: 8
              }

              size: 7
            }
          },

            show: false,
            padding: {
              top: 15,
              right: 7,
              left: 0,
              bottom: 0
            }
          },

          fill: {
            type: "gradient",

              shadeIntensity: 1,

          },

            show: false,

              show: false
            },

            },

        // Expenses Radial Chart

            }
          },
          colors: ["var(--color-warning)"],
          fill: {
            type: "gradient",
            gradient: {
              shadeIntensity: 1,
              opacityFrom: 0.4,
              gradientToColors: ["var(--color-base-100)"],
              opacityTo: 0.4,
              stops: [0, 85, 100]
            }
          },
          dataLabels: {
            enabled: false
          },
          stroke: {
            width: 2,
            curve: "straight"
          },
          series: [
            {
              name: "Sessions",
              data: [340, 340, 300, 300, 240, 240, 320, 320, 370]
            }
          ],
          xaxis: {
            show: false,
            lines: {
              show: false
            },
            labels: {
              show: false
            },
            axisBorder: {
              show: false
            }
          },
          yaxis: {
            show: false
          }
        }))

        // Profit Bar Chart
        buildChart("#profit-chart", () => ({
          chart: {
            type: "bar",
            height: 104,
            toolbar: {
              tools: {
                download: false
              }
            }
          },
          series: [
            {
              name: "Profit",
              data: [73, 73, 56, 56, 100]
            },
            {
              name: "Profit",
              data: [61, 61, 42, 74, 72]
            }
          ],
          plotOptions: {
            bar: {
              columnWidth: "75%",
              startingShape: "rounded",
              endingShape: "rounded",
              borderRadius: 3,
              dataLabels: {
                show: false
              }
            }
          },
          states: {
            hover: {
              filter: {
                type: "none"
              }
            },
            active: {
              filter: {
                type: "none"
              }
            }
          },
          grid: {
            show: false,
            padding: {
              top: -30,
              bottom: -12,
              left: 0,
              right: 0
            }
          },
          colors: ["var(--color-success)", "color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-success) 10%, transparent)"],
          dataLabels: {
            enabled: false
          },
          stroke: {
            show: true,
            width: 2,
            colors: ["var(--color-base-100)"]
          },
          legend: {
            show: false
          },
          xaxis: {
            categories: ["Jan", "Feb", "Mar", "Apr", "May"],
            axisBorder: {
              show: false
            },
            axisTicks: {
              show: false
            },
            labels: {
              style: {
                colors: "color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-base-content) 50%, transparent)",
                fontSize: "12px",
                fontFamily: "Inter, ui-sans-serif"
              }
            }
          },
          yaxis: {
            labels: {
              show: false
            }
          }
        }))

        // Sales Radial Chart
        buildChart("#sales-chart", () => ({
          chart: {
            height: 140,
            width: 140,
            sparkline: {
              enabled: true
            },
            parentHeightOffset: 0,
            type: "radialBar"
          },
          colors: ["var(--color-accent)"],
          series: [50],
          plotOptions: {
            radialBar: {
              startAngle: -90,
              endAngle: 90,
              hollow: {
                size: "60%"
              },
              track: {
                background: "color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-base-content) 10%, transparent)"
              },
              dataLabels: {
                name: {
                  show: false
                },
                value: {
                  fontSize: "18px",
                  fontFamily: "Inter, ui-sans-serif",
                  color: "var(--color-base-content)",
                  fontWeight: 600,
                  offsetY: -5
                }
              }
            }
          },
          stroke: {
            lineCap: "round"
          },
          grid: {
            show: false,
            padding: {
              left: 0,
              right: 0,
              top: 0,
              bottom: 3
            }
          },
          labels: ["Progress"]
        }))
      })()
    })
  </script>
  

</body>

  <script>
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
      const url = window.location.href

      // Check if the URL contains the "theme" parameter
      const themeParam = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("theme")
      const dirRTL = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("rtl")

      // If a theme parameter is present, apply it to the document
      if (themeParam) {
        document.documentElement.setAttribute("data-theme", themeParam)
      }
      if (dirRTL === "true") {
        document.documentElement.setAttribute("dir", "rtl")
      }
    })
  </script>

    <script>
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
      const url = window.location.href

      // Check if the URL contains the "theme" parameter
      const themeParam = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("theme")
      const dirRTL = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("rtl")

      // If a theme parameter is present, apply it to the document
      if (themeParam) {
        document.documentElement.setAttribute("data-theme", themeParam)
      }
      if (dirRTL === "true") {
        document.documentElement.setAttribute("dir", "rtl")
      }
    })
  </script>

  <script>
    window.addEventListener("load", () => {
      ;(function () {
        // Project Income Bar Chart
        buildChart("#project-income-chart", () => ({
          chart: {
            type: "bar",
            height: 180,
            toolbar: {
              tools: {
                download: false
              }
            }
          },
          series: [
            {
              name: "Income",
              data: [73, 73, 56, 73, 100]
            },
            {
              name: "Income",
              data: [73, 73, 56, 73, 100]
            }
          ],
          plotOptions: {
            bar: {
              columnWidth: "55%",
              startingShape: "rounded",
              endingShape: "rounded",
              borderRadius: 3,
              dataLabels: {
                show: false
              }
            }
          },
          states: {
            hover: {
              filter: {
                type: "none"
              }
            },
            active: {
              filter: {
                type: "none"
              }
            }
          },
          grid: {
            show: false,
            padding: {
              top: 0,
              bottom: -10,
              left: 0,
              right: 0
            }
          },
          colors: ["var(--color-success)", "color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-success) 10%, transparent)"],
          dataLabels: {
            enabled: false
          },
          stroke: {
            show: true,
            width: 2,
            colors: ["var(--color-base-100)"]
          },
          legend: {
            show: false
          },
          xaxis: {
            categories: ["Jan", "Feb", "Mar", "Apr", "May"],
            axisBorder: {
              show: false
            },
            axisTicks: {
              show: false
            },
            labels: {
              style: {
                colors: "color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-base-content) 50%, transparent)",
                fontSize: "12px",
                fontFamily: "Inter, ui-sans-serif"
              }
            }
          },
          yaxis: {
            labels: {
              show: false
            }
          }
        }))
      })()
    })
  </script>

    <script src="https://flyonui.becdn.net/pro/libs/flyonui/flyonui.js"></script>
    <script>
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
      const url = window.location.href

      // Check if the URL contains the "theme" parameter
      const themeParam = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("theme")
      const dirRTL = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("rtl")

      // If a theme parameter is present, apply it to the document
      if (themeParam) {
        document.documentElement.setAttribute("data-theme", themeParam)
      }
      if (dirRTL === "true") {
        document.documentElement.setAttribute("dir", "rtl")
      }
    })
  </script>

  <script>
    window.addEventListener("load", () => {
      ;(function () {
        // New Visitors Bar Chart
        buildChart("#new-visitors-chart", () => ({
          chart: {
            height: 120,
            width: 200,
            parentHeightOffset: 0,
            type: "bar",
            toolbar: {
              show: false
            }
          },
          plotOptions: {
            bar: {
              barHeight: "75%",
              columnWidth: "54%",
              startingShape: "rounded",
              endingShape: "rounded",
              borderRadius: 5,
              distributed: true
            }
          },
          grid: {
            show: false,
            padding: {
              top: -25,
              bottom: -12
            }
          },
          states: {
            hover: {
              filter: {
                type: "none"
              }
            },
            active: {
              filter: {
                type: "none"
              }
            }
          },
          colors: [
            "color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-primary) 10%, transparent)",
            "color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-primary) 10%, transparent)",
            "color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-primary) 10%, transparent)",
            "color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-primary) 10%, transparent)",
            "color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-primary) 10%, transparent)",
            "var(--color-primary)",
            "color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-primary) 10%, transparent)"
          ],
          dataLabels: {
            enabled: false
          },
          series: [
            {
              name: "Visitor",
              data: [40, 95, 60, 45, 90, 50, 75]
            }
          ],
          legend: {
            show: false
          },
          xaxis: {
            categories: ["S", "M", "T", "W", "T", "F", "S"],
            axisBorder: {
              show: false
            },
            axisTicks: {
              show: false
            },
            labels: {
              style: {
                colors: "color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-base-content) 50%, transparent)",
                fontSize: "13px",
                fontFamily: "Inter, ui-sans-serif"
              }
            }
          },
          yaxis: {
            labels: {
              show: false
            }
          }
        }))

        // Activity Area Chart
        buildChart("#activity-chart", () => ({
          chart: {
            height: 110,
            width: 220,
            parentHeightOffset: 0,
            toolbar: {
              show: false
            },
            type: "area"
          },
          dataLabels: {
            enabled: false
          },
          stroke: {
            width: 2,
            curve: "smooth"
          },
          series: [
            {
              name: "Activity",
              data: [15, 22, 17, 39, 12, 35, 25]
            }
          ],
          colors: ["var(--color-success)"],
          fill: {
            type: "gradient",
            gradient: {
              shadeIntensity: 1,
              opacityFrom: 0.7,
              gradientToColors: ["var(--color-base-100)"],
              opacityTo: 0.2,
              stops: [0, 85, 100]
            }
          },
          grid: {
            show: false,
            padding: {
              top: -20,
              bottom: -8
            }
          },
          legend: {
            show: false
          },
          xaxis: {
            categories: ["S", "M", "T", "W", "T", "F", "S"],
            axisBorder: {
              show: false
            },
            axisTicks: {
              show: false
            },
            labels: {
              style: {
                fontSize: "13px",
                colors: "color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-base-content) 50%, transparent)",
                fontFamily: "Inter, ui-sans-serif"
              }
            }
          },
          yaxis: {
            labels: {
              show: false
            }
          }
        }))
      })()
    })
  </script>
  

</body>

  <script>
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
      const url = window.location.href

      // Check if the URL contains the "theme" parameter
      const themeParam = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("theme")
      const dirRTL = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("rtl")

      // If a theme parameter is present, apply it to the document
      if (themeParam) {
        document.documentElement.setAttribute("data-theme", themeParam)
      }
      if (dirRTL === "true") {
        document.documentElement.setAttribute("dir", "rtl")
      }
    })
  </script>
  

</body>
